### 2026-10-06 22:01:23  #公司 #市場 #國際

【數十年高位收益率凸顯債券吸引力 Pimco強調分散配置】Pimco表示，債券收益率處於數十年來高位，為投資者提供了頗具吸引力的收益機會，投資者應將資產配置分散至發達市場和新興市場，以幫助防範財政風險。這家管理約2.33萬億美元資產的投資機構週二釋出最新的6至12個月週期展望。當前長期美國國債收益率徘徊在2002年以來最高水平附近，其中基準10年期美債收益率約為5.25%，30年期約為5.65%。Pimco經濟學家Tiffany Wilding和全球固定收益首席投資官Andrew Balls在報告中表示，“當前頗具吸引力的初始收益率以及由此帶來的利息收入，可以在相當程度上緩衝通脹尾部風險，同時仍保留債券在人工智慧資本支出推動力減弱或經濟增長遭遇衝擊時發揮對沖作用的潛力。”Pimco表示，投資者“在發達市場和新興市場之間進行全球債券組合配置，有助於分散包括財政風險在內的特定國家風險”。這家資產管理公司表示，美國和法國“尤其突出，兩國的債務路徑更具挑戰性”。與此同時，“英國、義大利和日本仍然較為脆弱 —— 鑑於近期政策進一步擴大赤字，日本的脆弱性有所上升 —— 但按照目前的財政計劃，這些國家的債務路徑看來仍具有可持續性。”

【以色列政府發出旅行警告 提醒海外公民防範潛在安全風險】當地時間6日，以色列總理辦公室透過社交媒體釋出官方通知稱，在2023年10月7日新一輪巴以衝突爆發三週年來臨之際，提醒海外以色列公民提高安全防範意識，以應對潛在的襲擊風險和示威活動。通知稱，相關安全評估顯示，隨著衝突爆發週年的臨近，海外針對以色列和猶太人目標的襲擊圖謀風險有所上升。部分地區武裝組織和極端勢力將這一節點視為其開展活動的標誌性時刻。通知為此向公眾提出多項防範建議，包括避免前往約旦、卡達、埃及（含西奈半島）等被以色列列入高風險旅行警告的國家和地區；在人員密集場所儘量減少使用希伯來語；避免在社交網路上公開海外行程或上傳涉及其安全部隊服役經歷的內容。此外，通知還呼籲海外公民遠離各地的示威與集會活動。（央視新聞）

【標普500指數創紀錄新高 油價回落且AI交易熱情再度升溫】油價回落推動美股升至歷史新高，華爾街也受到人工智慧交易熱情再度升溫的提振。AI熱潮一直是本輪牛市的重要推動力。紐約時間9:33，標普500指數和納斯達克100指數上漲0.5%，道指走高0.4%。美股升勢延續至連續第四個交易日，標普500指數兩個月來首次觸及紀錄新高。英偉達市值逼近6萬億美元。AMD執行長蘇姿豐預計，未來幾年晶片需求將“非常高”。布倫特原油跌至每桶98美元，緩解了市場對通脹的擔憂，並推動美國國債收益率小幅下行。儘管能源成本和利率帶來的風險不斷上升，美股表現仍出人意料地堅挺，市場波動也維持在較低水平。瑞銀首席投資辦公室的Ulrike Hoffmann-Burchardi表示，美國經濟仍對企業營收和利潤構成支撐。她表示：“我們仍然高度看好AI增長前景，並認為AI相關投資仍是整體股市的強勁推動力。良好的增長環境和穩健的企業盈利表明，利潤增長有望從科技行業擴充套件至更多板塊。”22V Research董事Kevin Brocks表示：“只要未來幾周我們從企業財報以及Anthropic的IPO申報檔案中獲得的關於AI投資回報率(ROI)的新增資訊保持積極，股指面臨的風險就很小。近期宏觀經濟資料顯示，經濟陷入衰退的風險很低，美聯儲從當前水平進一步收緊金融狀況的風險也不大。”

TSX Edges Higher as Oil Prices and Bond Yields Ease — The S&P/TSX Composite Index edged higher near 35,500 on Tuesday amid easing oil prices and a pullback in global bond yields. Oil prices fell as resilient Middle Eastern crude exports and a G7 emergency stockpile release eased supply concerns, reducing energy-driven inflation pressures. Yields also eased from multi-year highs, offering some respite to credit-sensitive markets. RBC and TD Bank rose more than 0.5%, while BMO, Scotiabank and CIBC gained nearly 1%. Miners were mostly lower amid volatile gold prices, with Barrick and WPM shedding about 0.5%. Elsewhere, Jamieson Wellness was near flat after receiving final court approval for its pending acquisition by Kirin Holdings. On the data front, Canada’s merchandise trade surplus widened to a four-year high in August as US consumers front-loaded orders before new tariffs took effect at the end of the month.

### 2026-10-06 21:46:30  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Silver Holds Above $61 as Fed Rate-Hike Bets Fade — Silver hovered around $61 an ounce on Tuesday as fading expectations of a Federal Reserve rate hike this month offset pressure from a firm US dollar and elevated Treasury yields. Markets have scaled back expectations for an October rate increase following weaker-than-expected job growth in September and downward revisions to payrolls for the previous two months. Traders now see only about a 20% chance of a rate hike this month, while pricing in nearly a 70% probability of an increase in December, according to the CME FedWatch Tool. Meanwhile, the dollar remained close to an 18-month high, supported by rising government debt, widening budget deficits and political uncertainty across parts of the Eurozone, which have weighed on the euro. Elsewhere, oil prices fell on Tuesday as resilient Middle Eastern crude exports and a G7 emergency stockpile release eased concerns over potential supply disruptions.

Gold Slightly Up as Fed Rate-Hike Bets Fade — Gold edged above $4,160 an ounce on Tuesday as fading expectations of a Federal Reserve rate hike this month offset pressure from a firm US dollar and elevated Treasury yields. Markets have scaled back bets on an October rate hike following weaker-than-expected job growth in September and downward revisions to payrolls for the previous two months. Traders now see only about a 20% chance of a rate increase this month, while still pricing in nearly a 70% probability of a hike in December, according to the CME FedWatch Tool. Higher interest rates raise the opportunity cost of holding non-yielding gold. The dollar, however, remained close to an 18-month high, supported by rising government debt, widening budget deficits and political uncertainty in parts of the Eurozone, which have weighed on the euro. Meanwhile, oil prices fell on Tuesday as resilient Middle Eastern crude exports and a G7 emergency stockpile release eased concerns over supply disruptions.

Ibovespa Extends Election-Driven Rally — The Ibovespa rose around 0.5% to near the 208,000 mark on Tuesday after closing at a record 206,912 the previous session, as Flávio Bolsonaro finished ahead of President Lula in the first election round, defying most polls. The result triggered a sharp repricing of Brazilian assets, with Bolsonaro receiving 47.03% of valid votes versus 45.16% for Lula. Markets view the senator as more fiscally restrictive amid elevated domestic yields and weak business activity. Morgan Stanley upgraded Brazilian stocks to Overweight from Neutral. Financials extended gains after surging as much as 22% the previous session, with Itaú up 2% and Bradesco gaining more than 3.5%. Other notable gainers included Rede D’Or (+2%) and Sabesp (+1.5%). Supporting equities, Brazil’s 10-year bond yield fell below 13%, while global yields also eased from multi-year highs as oil prices pulled back.

### 2026-10-06 20:56:47  #市場 #觀點

【摩根士丹利：大選投票後將巴西股票上調至超配】週日總統大選投票後，市場預期巴西將推進財政整頓，摩根士丹利將巴西股票評級上調至超配。弗拉維奧・博索納羅意外領跑，帶動巴西各類資產普漲。分析師尼古拉・利普曼、朱莉婭・諾蓋拉、馬科斯・里奧斯在研報中寫道，他們預計巴西股市到年底最多可上漲 20%；若財政政策轉向，伊博韋斯帕指數有望觸及 25 萬點，2027 年將進一步上行。週一，伊博韋斯帕指數大漲 7.7%，創 2020 年 3 月以來最大單日漲幅。“巴西原有財政模式已經難以為繼，” 分析師稱，“大選開啟了新路徑的可能性：財政整頓疊加國內經濟放緩，最終將擠出債務、吸引投資。”分析師指出，核心在於依託財政整頓，推動市場資金從債市轉向股票與實體投資。本土投資者此前因高利率創紀錄，被擠出股市。巴西股票自由流通市值約 5000 億美元。摩根士丹利估算，本土投資者可新增 300–400 億美元買盤，新興市場投資者可新增 100–200 億美元，全球投資者可新增 300–400 億美元。

US Imports Hit Fresh Record High — US imports surged $17.2 billion to an all-time high of $420.8 billion in August 2026, contributing to a widening in the trade deficit to $105.6 billion from a revised $92.8 billion in July. The increase was led by a $9.1 billion rise in industrial supplies and materials, mainly crude oil and nonmonetary gold, while capital goods imports rose $6.2 billion on stronger purchases of semiconductors and other industrial machinery. These gains more than offset a decline in computer accessories. Services imports were broadly unchanged, as higher transport imports offset declines in charges for the use of intellectual property and travel.

### 2026-10-06 20:47:03  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Imports #Importance 1

Canada Imports End Rising Streak — Canada’s goods imports fell 2% month over month to C$73.7 billion in August 2026, marking the first decline since January. Despite the overall decrease, six of 11 product sections recorded gains. Imports of motor vehicles and parts fell 8.8%, while passenger cars and light trucks dropped 15.4% after reaching an all-time high in July due to shorter seasonal production stoppages in the US. Imports of metal and non-metallic mineral products declined 7.0%, led by a 40% drop in unwrought gold, silver and platinum group metals and their alloys, reflecting lower purchases of gold held in Canada by US residents. Imports of metal ores and non-metallic minerals fell 15.5% to their lowest level since November 2025, led by a 17.1% decline in other metal ores and concentrates. Lower imports of lead and zinc ores from Australia and Peru, as well as gold, silver and platinum group metal ores from Brazil, Peru and Mauritania, contributed to the decline.

### 2026-10-06 20:46:57  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Balance of Trade #Importance 2

Canada Trade Surplus Rises to 4-Year High — Canada recorded a trade surplus of C$4.2 billion in August of 2026, widening from the upwardly revised C$0.79 billion in the earlier month to mark the largest trade surplus since May of 2022 and the second-largest since August 2008, despite the appreciation of the Canadian dollar against the greenback. Exports jumped by 2.5% from the previous month to C$77.9 billion as the increase in energy prices due to the war in the Middle East drove energy product sales to surge 4.7%. Exports of consumer goods rose 6.6% and those of industrial machinery and equipment rose 10.1%. Exports to the US surged 8.1% as US consumers likely front-loaded purchases of Canadian goods before a series of tariffs took effect later in the month. Meanwhile, imports fell by 2.0% to C$73.7 billion. Purchases were lower for motor vehicles and parts (-8.8%) and metal and non-metallic mineral products (-7.0%), mainly due to a plunge in unwrought gold, silver, and platinum group metals (-40.0%).

### 2026-10-06 20:37:38  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Exports #Importance 1

Canada Exports Rebound in August — Canada’s goods exports rose 2.5% month-over-month to C$77.9 billion in August 2026, rebounding from a downwardly revised 2.6% decline in July. Gains were recorded in eight of 11 product sections. Energy exports increased 4.7%, marking the first rise since April. Refined petroleum exports posted the largest monthly gain at 17.4%, driven by higher diesel shipments to Peru, the UK, US and Netherlands. Crude oil exports rose 2.1% on higher prices, while nuclear fuel and other energy product exports jumped 58.4%, driven by increased shipments to Germany and the UK. Consumer goods exports rose 6.6%, led by a 43.3% increase in miscellaneous goods and supplies, partly due to higher shipments of gold and silver coins to the US. Industrial machinery, equipment and parts exports rose 10.1%, their highest level since January 2025, while electronic and electrical equipment and parts increased 11%.

US Trade Deficit Widens to 17-Month High — The US trade deficit widened to $105.6 billion in August 2026 from a revised $92.8 billion in July, exceeding market expectations of $102.0 billion and marking its largest gap since March 2025. The widening was driven by a $17.2 billion surge in imports to an all-time high of $420.8 billion. Imports of industrial supplies and materials rose $9.1 billion, led by crude oil and nonmonetary gold, while capital goods imports increased $6.2 billion on stronger purchases of semiconductors and other industrial machinery. These gains more than offset a decline in computer accessories. 【商務部新聞發言人就法德要求歐盟強化貿易防禦等保護主義工具答記者問 】
問：據媒體報道，10月5日法國和德國向歐委會提交非正式檔案，要求歐盟強化使用貿易防禦措施，並推出多項保護主義新工具。請問中方對此有何評論？
答：中方注意到有關報道。中方始終認為，相互依賴不是風險，利益交融不是威脅，開放合作才是發展的正道。保護主義提升不了競爭力，脫鉤斷鏈只會損人不利己。希望法國和德國作為全球重要經濟體，能夠堅持開放合作和自由貿易，遵守世貿規則，不要鼓動歐盟動輒使用保護主義工具，更不要將經貿問題政治化和泛安全化，避免以錯誤方式走錯誤道路並最終反噬自己。

Mexico Consumer Sentiment Weakens in September — Mexico’s consumer confidence fell 1.2 points to 45.1 in September 2026 from August, retreating from its highest level in a year, while sentiment remained negative. All five components deteriorated on the month. The sharpest decline was in households’ assessment of their current economic situation, which fell 1.9 points to 52. Expectations for households’ economic situation over the next 12 months dropped 1.5 points to 57.6. Assessments of the country’s current economic situation declined 1.3 points to 39, while expectations for the country’s economic outlook fell 0.9 point. Households’ willingness to purchase durable goods, such as furniture and appliances, dropped 0.5 point to 32. Year over year, consumer confidence declined 1.0 point.

US Futures Rise to Records — Futures tracking US equities were firmly higher on Tuesday with support from the heavyweight tech sector, as the surge in bond yields paused and softened pressure on the sector's credit outlook. Contracts for the S&P 500 and the Nasdaq 100 were up around 0.5% at record highs, while those for the Dow added 0.4%. Both AI infrastructure and hyperscaler stocks were firmly up premarket as fresh deals in the sector continued to spur speculative demand for the technology. OpenAI was in talks for a $30 billion round of financing with UAE funds and Alphabet was nearly 1% higher ahead of an agreement to acquire nuclear energy capacity from Constellation Energy, which rose 7%. ### 2026-10-06 19:41:22  #Trading Economics #Economy #Latvia #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Latvia Industrial Output Returns to Growth — Latvia's industrial production rose 0.3% year-on-year in August 2026, rebounding from a 0.2% decline in July. Output growth accelerated in mining and quarrying (36.1% vs 12.4%), while manufacturing rose at a softer pace (7.6% vs 8.2%), as gains eased for food products (8.9% vs 11.7%), fabricated metal products (12.3% vs 33.9%), computer and electronic products (20.4% vs 23.4%) and machinery and equipment (17.5% vs 36.9%), and output fell for beverages (-18.4% vs -5.2%) and motor vehicles (-10.2% vs 11.8%). In contrast, production picked up for repair and installation of machinery (20.2% vs 8.2%) and chemicals (11.5% vs 8.1%). Meanwhile, activity declined at a steeper pace in electricity and gas supply (-44.6% vs -35.6%). On a monthly basis, industrial production rose 0.3% in August, following a 0.1% increase in July. In the first eight months of the year, output increased 4.7% from a year earlier.

### 2026-10-06 19:21:14  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

10-Year Treasury Yield Softens from 24-Year High — The yield on the 10-year US Treasury note was at 5.27% on Tuesday, pausing a surge that peaked at a 24-year high of 5.33% touched yesterday, tracking the momentary dip in wholesale fuel prices. Oil bids were tamed by signs that some laden tankers passed through the Strait of Hormuz. The 10-year yield has surged over 110bps this year as risks of higher rates from the Federal Reserve, soaring federal deficit spending, and increasing corporate debt supply triggered a plunge in demand for duration. The price gauge in the ISM Services PMI surged to a four-year high, consolidating expectations that the Fed will deliver another rate hike this year. Meanwhile, a widening budget deficit raised the outlook for the supply of Treasury securities despite the Treasury's preference to service its increasing debt with coupon-less bills. The latest figures from the CBO showed net public debt rising from 101% of GDP this year to over 175% by 2056, assuming a 10-year yield below 4.5%.

【谷歌與Constellation達成為期20年的核電採購協議】谷歌母公司Alphabet Inc.已同意與Constellation Energy Corp.簽署一項為期20年的核電採購協議。在資料中心建設熱潮推動電力需求激增之際，科技公司正競相鎖定長期電力供應。兩家公司週二在宣告中表示，這項協議涉及890兆瓦新增電力，證實了此前的報道。Constellation將為擴建專案投入超過43億美元。新增電力將接入美國最大的輸電運營商PJM Interconnection的電網。人工智慧熱潮需要消耗大量電力，正推動市場重新關注核能。核電既能全天候穩定供電，也不會產生碳排放。近期交易主要圍繞現有核電設施展開，因為新建反應堆從審批到建成通常需要十年甚至更久，而下一代裂變核能技術距離大規模部署可能仍需數年。亞馬遜上週也與Constellation達成類似協議，這家雲及電商公司同意購買690兆瓦電力，其中包括透過升級馬里蘭州Calvert Cliffs核電設施所增加的發電能力。

### 2026-10-06 18:02:04  #Trading Economics #Markets #United Kingdom #Currency #Importance 1

Sterling Holds Above $1.32 as UK Inflation Concerns Persist — Sterling traded slightly above $1.32 as elevated energy costs and persistent inflation concerns reinforced expectations for UK interest rates to remain higher for longer. BoE policymaker Catherine Mann said on Tuesday that inflation above the Bank’s 2% target appeared to have become embedded in the economy. She warned that inflation could reach 4% around the turn of the year, when wage negotiations typically take place, potentially adding to price pressures. Mann has been among a minority on the MPC voting for a 25-bp rate hike to 4% since July. Meanwhile, weaker-than-expected US PCE inflation and jobs data last week strengthened expectations that the Federal Reserve could leave rates unchanged in October, with a December move now seen as more likely. ### 2026-10-06 18:01:10  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #LMI Logistics Managers Index #Importance 2

US Logistics Growth 2nd-Highest in 4 Years — The US Logistics Managers’ Index increased to 70.2 in September 2026 from 66.6 in August. It is the second-highest reading in four years, behind only June’s reading of 71.1. A faster increase was seen for Inventory Levels (58.9 vs 52.8) and Inventory Costs (79.9 vs 78.6) which is also having a significant effect on the warehousing market. Warehousing Capacity shrank the most since March 2022 (39.3 vs 53.5), potentially due to the fact that firms, particularly retailers, had run inventory down a bit and are not rushing goods in now ahead of potential holiday sales. The reduction in available storage capacity has boosted Warehousing Utilization (64.3 vs 59.6) and is likely behind the consistently robust expansion of Warehousing Prices (73.5 vs 75). There are similar dynamics in Transportation Capacity (34.4 vs 40) while Transportation Utilization continues expanding but at a slower rate (66.1 vs 70.6). Finally, Transportation Prices continue to surge (92.7 vs 90).

Crude Oil at Over 1-Month Low — Crude oil fell for a third consecutive session on Tuesday, approaching $87 a barrel, the lowest since early September, amid signs that Middle Eastern crude exports are recovering. Saudi Arabia’s Energy Minister said that oil flows through the East-West Pipeline reached 5.8 million barrels, while Reuters reported that shipments through the route had not been interrupted. In addition, reports showed that crude exports from the Middle East exceeded pre-war levels on four days during the final week of September. Adding to the bearish pressure, G7 countries agreed on Friday to release 100 million barrels of diesel and crude from emergency reserves and pledged to refrain from imposing energy export restrictions. Saudi Arabia also sharply cut the price of its flagship crude grade for Asian buyers as supply flows improved, providing further evidence of a loosening market. Meanwhile, OPEC+ agreed to keep its oil production targets unchanged for November.

Eurozone Retail Sales Inch Higher — Retail sales volumes in the Eurozone inched higher by 0.1% from the previous month in August of 2026, loosely aligned with market expectations of a 0.2% increase and trimming the 0.6% drop in July, which was the sharpest decline in over one year. Sales were sharply higher for core non-food products (0.5% vs -1.2% in July) to reflect some fresh resilience to higher borrowing costs and prices, while food, drinks, and tobacco sales also inched up (0.1% vs -0.1%). In turn, automotive fuel sales volumes plunged 1.9%, extending the 0.2% drop in the earlier period as the surge in fuel prices drove consumers to cut back on tank refills. From the previous year, Eurozone retail sales jumped by 0.8%, rebounding from the two-year low increase of 0.4% in July.

Bund Yields Retreat as ECB Rate-Hike Bets Ease — Germany’s 10-year Bund yield fell to 3.45%, retreating from the 17-year highs reached last week, as turmoil in bond markets prompted investors to scale back expectations for further ECB rate hikes. Markets are now pricing in an 80% probability of another rate increase by year-end. Whereas investors had previously expected at least three further hikes by March 2027, they are now fully pricing in just one additional move, with roughly an 80% chance of a second. ### 2026-10-06 16:53:39  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Construction PMI #Importance 1

UK Construction Activity Falls Less in September — The S&P Global UK Construction PMI rose to 46.1 in September 2026 from 44.3 in August, missing expectations of 45.2 and signaling the slowest decline in construction output since January. Commercial construction showed the greatest resilience, falling only marginally, followed by civil engineering and residential construction with softer contractions. However, total new orders fell at the fastest pace since June amid sluggish market conditions, delayed decisions on major projects, and sharply rising input costs. Employment fell again, with job cuts accelerating the fastest in five months. Demand for construction products and materials also declined, while delivery times lengthened to the greatest extent since May amid shipping delays and supply chain disruptions related to the Middle East conflict. Meanwhile, input cost inflation eased to a seven-month low. Construction firms remained optimistic about the year ahead, although confidence fell to its lowest since May.

### 2026-10-06 15:56:46  #Trading Economics #Economy #Italy #Construction PMI #Importance 1

Italy Construction Downturn Eases in September — The S&P Global Italy Construction PMI rose to 46.5 in September 2026, picking up from a four-year low of 41.7 in the previous month. The improvement was driven by a less marked decline in new construction orders, while employment also recorded a modest increase. Meanwhile, lead times continued to lengthen, reflecting the impact of the war in the Middle East, with logistical and transport disruptions and stock shortages weighing on supply-chain performance. On the price front, input-cost inflation accelerated to a four-month high, driven by higher outlays for raw materials, fuel, transport and energy. Among subsectors, all remained in contraction, with the steepest decline recorded in housing, while commercial construction signaled only a slight contraction. Looking ahead, Italian construction companies remained optimistic about output over the next 12 months, although confidence stayed below its long-run average.

### 2026-10-06 15:50:19  #Trading Economics #Economy #France #Construction PMI #Importance 1

French Construction Sector Contracts Less in September — The S&P Global France Construction PMI rose to 39.8 in September 2026 from 37.3 in August, remaining well below the 50 threshold, signaling a continued sector-wide downturn. Commercial construction was once again the weakest segment, posting a steep decline followed by residential construction, while civil engineering activity posted its slowest contraction in eight months. New projects continued to fall, extending the downturn in demand to four-and-a-half years, amid a lack of new tenders, client budget constraints, and general uncertainty. Firms also cut construction material purchases and employment, extending the current period of job shedding that began in May 2024. Meanwhile, input cost inflation remained historically elevated, partly due to repercussions from the war in the Middle East, while delivery delays eased to their lowest since June 2025. French constructors also became more pessimistic about the year ahead, with expectations falling to their weakest since December 2025.

French Stocks Rebound — The CAC 40 rose 0.4% to 7,863 on Tuesday, rebounding from the previous session even as concerns over France’s high debt levels and political uncertainty lingered. As investors continued to shed French assets, Prime Minister Sébastien Lecornu’s government sought to ease market jitters by unveiling its 2027 budget. Far-right presidential candidate Marine Le Pen also proposed cutting government spending by €25 billion a year, seeking to strengthen her fiscal credibility as concerns over France’s public finances and bond-market pressures mount. The state budget deficit widened to €159.6 billion in January-August from €157.5 billion a year earlier, as expenditure rose 4.4% to €362.97 billion while revenue increased 3.1% to €228.55 billion. 【Lithuanian parliament backs repeal of constitutional nuclear-weapons ban in first-round vote】Lithuania's parliament on Tuesday approved in a first-round vote a proposal to repeal the constitutional ban on nuclear weapons, the first of two votes required for the change to take effect. The 141-seat parliament recorded 106 votes in favour. The amendment, which would abolish a ban imposed more than 30 years ago, must secure a two-thirds majority in both parliamentary votes; the second round is scheduled for Jan. 12. President Nausėda said parties had agreed the plan in July and that there is currently no plan to host nuclear weapons, but removing the clause would allow deployment if the security situation changes. Lawmakers framed the move as a reassessment of regional security prompted by Russia's actions.

Germany Construction Activity Falls at Faster Pace — The S&P Global Germany Construction PMI Total Activity Index fell to 43.5 in September 2026 from August’s eight-month high of 48.7. Renewed weakness in housing activity was the notable drag, with activity recording its steepest rate of decline since December 2024 after showing signs of steadying in August, while commercial activity also suffered a fresh setback following a brief return to growth. Meanwhile, civil engineering activity fell marginally. New orders continued to decline, albeit with the rate of contraction easing to its weakest in three months. This led constructors to scale back purchases of building materials and products, while the latest reduction in employment was the most pronounced since April. Cost pressures also intensified, with faster increases in both average input costs and subcontractor rates. Looking ahead, constructors remained pessimistic about the outlook for activity over the next 12 months.

Eurozone Construction Activity Remains Weak — The S&P Global Eurozone Construction PMI rose slightly to 43.4 in September 2026 from 43.0 in August, but remained below the 50.0 threshold, signaling a marked contraction in construction activity. The downturn remained broad-based across France, Germany and Italy, with France recording the steepest contraction, Germany seeing a much sharper decline and Italy the slowest fall. Meanwhile, new orders continued to fall, extending the downturn in demand to four-and-a-half years, although the pace eased from August. 【華為徐直軍：Peerium架構與靈衢匯流排 讓百萬顆處理器成為一臺計算機】在上海舉行的華為全聯接（HC）大會期間，華為輪值董事長徐直軍與海思首席科學家廖恆，就華為新發布的AI時代的Peerium計算架構和靈衢匯流排（UnifiedBus），與媒體記者進行了問答交流。 今年HC大會期間，華為展出了昇騰950和Atlas 950超節點。徐直軍表示，“超節點”如今被廣泛提及，但不同廠商實現差異很大，真正的挑戰在於讓數千、數萬顆處理器組成一臺計算機。華為將這一全新計算架構命名為Peerium，它能讓百萬顆處理器成為一臺計算機。目前，華為正部署和測試25.6萬張卡規模的Atlas 950 SuperPoD超節點。 廖恆認為，未來兩年內，預計中國將出現約6至7個前沿AI實驗室，目標都是訓練引數規模在10萬億到40萬億之間的基礎語言模型。這些數字與超節點的記憶體容量和規模大致匹配，因此需要如此規模的基礎設施來做訓練。（科創板日報）

【世界銀行上調東亞和太平洋地區今年經濟增速預測】世界銀行6日釋出東亞和太平洋地區經濟預測更新報告，將2026年該地區經濟增速預測上調至4.5%，較4月預測高0.3個百分點。報告表示，這一經濟增速上調很大程度上是由於作為人工智慧價值鏈一部分的國家高技術投資和出口表現強勁。在市場力量、可再生能源使用增加、動用庫存和消費者補貼等因素共同作用下，高能源價格的拖累沒有預期那樣嚴重。世界銀行表示，在高技術產品製造和出口推動下，該地區多個經濟體今年經濟增速超出此前預期。其中，越南、馬來西亞和泰國今年經濟增速預測被分別上調1.1個百分點、0.7個百分點和0.7個百分點。對高能源價格敏感和對外部衝擊緩衝有限的太平洋島國今年經濟增速預測被下調0.5個百分點。（新華社）

Spain Industrial Production Growth Slows in August — Industrial production in Spain increased by 1.5% year-on-year in August 2026, following an upwardly revised 2.5% increase in July. Energy production recorded the strongest growth, rising by 4.7%, followed by intermediate goods (2%) and capital goods (0.9%). By contrast, consumer goods production declined by 1%. Durable consumer goods was the only sector to post positive monthly growth, at 1%, while energy recorded the sharpest decline, falling by 2%.

敞開大門，讓民營企業“進得去”。我們正在完善民營企業參與重大專案建設長效機制，推進基礎設施競爭性領域向經營主體公平開放。不同體量、專長的企業，都能在這張網上找到自己的位置。

The head of equity capital markets at a large U.S. asset manager said: "Nobody cares about any company other than Anthropic right now." Investment bankers' strong fee income this year has largely reflected SpaceX's June IPO. Bankers said fears of an AI downturn and public opposition to data centers have left the IPO market essentially stalled in recent weeks. Investors are increasingly questioning out-of-touch valuations assigned to AI-linked companies. BCA Research shows tech companies that listed this year have fallen about 23% on average since their first trading day, adding to concerns that banks may have priced deals too aggressively to win mandates.

### 2026-10-06 15:17:38  #Trading Economics #Economy #France #Government Budget Value #Importance 1

France Budget Deficit Widens in Jan-Aug — France’s state budget deficit widened to EUR 159.6 billion in the January-August period from EUR 157.5 billion a year earlier. Expenditure rose 4.4% year-on-year to EUR 362.97 billion, driven mainly by higher debt-servicing costs amid rising interest rates and inflation, increased military spending, and higher public energy service charges (CSPE). Meanwhile, general budget revenue increased 3.1% to EUR 228.55 billion. Net tax revenue rose to EUR 206 billion, supported by higher net personal income tax, the State’s share of net VAT revenue, net corporate income tax, and other net tax revenue. Non-tax revenue increased to EUR 18.9 billion, mainly due to the return to the State in January 2026 of EUR 6.9 billion in non-consumable endowments allocated under the Future Investment Programs (PIA) in 2010 and 2014, which had reached maturity. Special Treasury accounts posted a EUR 25.2 billion deficit, an improvement of EUR 6.3 billion from the end of August 2025.

Hungary Retail Sales Growth Slows to Over 1-Year Low — Retail sales in Hungary rose by 2.4% year-on-year in August 2026, easing from a 4.9% increase in the previous month. This reading marked the weakest growth since May 2025, as sales moderated in food products (0.5% vs 2.2% in July), with lower activity in non-specialized food and beverages (1.9% vs 3.6%) and a continued decline in food, beverages and tobacco (-4.8% vs -2.2%). Sales of non-food products also slowed (5.0% vs 6.5%), including manufactured goods in non-specialized shops (4.2% vs 5.1%), textiles, clothing and footwear (2.7% vs 5.4%), furniture and electrical goods (1.0% vs 5.2%), books, computer equipment and other goods (0.6% vs 5.9%), and pharmaceutical and medical goods and cosmetic articles (5.4% vs 9.2%). Automotive fuel sales also rose at a softer pace (3.1% vs 8.1%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, retail trade fell by 0.7% in August, following a 1.0% rise in the previous month.

Switzerland Jobless Rate Steady at 3% — Switzerland’s non-seasonally adjusted unemployment rate was 3.0% in September 2026, unchanged from the previous two months. The number of unemployed people fell by 1,064 from a month earlier to 140,480. Across the cantons, the largest decreases were recorded in Zurich (-355), St. Gallen (-224), Aargau (-204), Freiburg (-185), and Waadt (-147). The drop in unemployment was limited to the industry (-548) and services (-546) sectors. The jobless rate among young people aged 15 to 24 edged down to 3.3% from a five-and-a-half-year high of 3.4% in August, with the number of unemployed youth falling by 332 to 14,346. The unemployment rate for workers aged 50 to 64 also ticked lower to 2.6% from 2.7%, as the number of unemployed people in this age group decreased by 201 to 38,482. Registered job seekers rose by 466 to 44,199. Seasonally adjusted, the jobless rate was unchanged at 3.1% for the fifth month in a row.

This marked the highest reading since April, as price growth accelerated for energy (5.9% vs 2.3% in August) and goods (1.2% vs 0.2%), while it also edged up for services (4.6% vs 4.5%). At the same time, deflation eased for food and soft drinks (-3.5% vs -4.3%). Meanwhile, inflation eased slightly for alcoholic beverages and tobacco (4.6% vs 4.7%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices were unchanged in September after rising 0.3% in August, marking the lowest reading since June.

Meanwhile, Prime Minister Sanae Takaichi continued to pursue expansionary fiscal measures despite concerns surrounding the weak yen and rising domestic bond yields. In corporate developments, Toyota Motor Corp. is turning to its new Land Cruiser FJ to strengthen its position against intensifying competition from Chinese automakers, with sales across Japan and Southeast Asia exceeding initial expectations. ### 2026-10-06 15:06:49  #Trading Economics #Economy #Austria #Wholesale Prices YoY #Importance 1

Austria Wholesale Inflation Highest Since 2023 — Wholesale prices in Austria climbed by 10.2% year-on-year in September 2026, marking the fastest rise since February 2023 and accelerating from an 8.2% gain in the previous month. The increase was driven by sharp price gains in other liquid and gaseous fuels and related products (68.8%), motor spirit (45.1%), plastics and rubber in primary forms (26.6%), waste and scrap (25.6%), and non-iron metals (21.2%). Prices also rose for information and communication equipment (18.8%), other machinery and equipment (18.1%), solid fuels (16.2%), grain, seeds and animal feeds (11.8%), and iron and steel (11.8%). In contrast, prices fell for live animals (-10.9%) and dairy products, eggs and edible oils and fats (-1.3%). On a monthly basis, wholesale prices rose 1.9% in September, the highest in six months and accelerating from 0.9% in August, mainly due to higher prices for other liquid and gaseous fuels and related products (12.6%), motor spirit (10.4%), and grain, seeds and animal feeds (7.0%).

### 2026-10-06 15:02:20  #Trading Economics #Markets #Indonesia #Currency #Importance 1

Rupiah Gains Capped by Dollar Strength, External Risks — The Indonesian rupiah hovered around IDR 17,910 per U.S. dollar on Tuesday, edging higher from the previous session as traders awaited key domestic releases later this week, including September forex reserves, consumer sentiment, and August retail sales. Meanwhile, fiscal conditions through August were supported by revenue growth, even as tax-restitution delays reportedly persisted. The upside remained limited, however, as Indonesia's external position stayed vulnerable, with elevated imports and energy costs weighing on the balance amid persistent Middle East headwinds. Inflation could also become harder to contain in the coming months as food prices stay elevated following supply disruptions linked to El Niño. On the global front, the dollar index lingered near an 18-month high, lifted by elevated U.S. Treasury yields and higher oil prices, while euro weakness amid political and fiscal concerns in the eurozone further boosted demand for the greenback, capping further rupiah gains.

### 2026-10-06 14:57:50  #Trading Economics #Economy #France #Industrial Production Mom #Importance 2

France Industrial Output Surprisingly Falls — Industrial production in France fell 0.3% month-on-month in August 2026, defying expectations for a 0.3% rise and following a 0.6% decline in July. The drop was mainly driven by a 2.4% decline in extractive industries, energy and water, which more than offset a 0.3% increase in manufacturing. The energy decline was largely due to lower electricity production after output reached its highest level since December 2021 in July. Within manufacturing, output of other industrial products rose 1.2%, while electrical, electronic and computer equipment increased 0.9%. Coking and refining output also rose 3.3%. In contrast, transport equipment manufacturing fell 2.2%, while agri-food production dropped 1.2%. On a yearly basis, industrial output rose 0.3%, rebounding from a 0.6% fall in July. Over the three months to August, industrial production was down 0.1% from a year earlier.

### 2026-10-06 14:57:19  #公司 #市場

【Reflection AI正式釋出Beam 對標主流開源模型】Reflection AI正式推出其首款前沿開源權重模型Beam。這家成立兩年、總部位於布魯克林的初創企業稱，Beam在高階推理基準測試上效能對標主流開源模型，同時成本大幅降低。企業週一在一篇長篇部落格中披露更多細節，稱Beam為純文字混合專家模型，基於高算力強化學習訓練，在推理、程式碼編寫與智慧體任務上具備出色能力，“token成本與推理算力僅為競品的一小部分”。Beam總引數量5010億，啟用引數量230億。預訓練使用23.8萬億token，上下文視窗達100萬token。Reflection宣稱的效能尚未經過獨立驗證，但該公司表示，在高階推理基準測試中，Beam得分與Z.ai的GLM-5.2相當，且優於當前主流西方開源模型，同時推理算力消耗“低3至4倍”。Reflection將其定位為面向企業、公共部門與開發者的“主力模型”。

TAIEX Ends at Record High, Nears 49,900 — The TAIEX, Taiwan’s benchmark stock index, rose 111 points, or 0.2%, to a fresh record peak of 49,823 on Tuesday, extending gains for a fifth consecutive session, buoyed by gains in financials, process industries, and technology services. The broader index closed above the 49,000 level for the second straight session, tracking an upbeat session on Wall Street overnight, driven by a rally in tech stocks. Traders sought to capitalize on the momentum, with the financial sector rising 1.2%, mainly supported by gains in Fubon Financial Holding (1.6%), Cathay Financial Holding (0.5%), and CTBC Financial Holding (1.5%). Meanwhile, process industries climbed 1.4%, with Nan Ya Plastics Corp. rising 2.5%, while technology services surged 3.5%, boosted by a 6.5% jump in Wiwynn Corp. By contrast, electronic technology edged down 0.1%; however, TSMC, the world's largest contract chipmaker and accounting for more than 40% of the market's total value, rose 0.4%.

### 2026-10-06 14:38:32  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hungary #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Hungary Industrial Output Growth Accelerates in August — Industrial production in Hungary grew by 8.7% year-on-year in August 2026, accelerating from a 4.7% rise in the previous month. The increase was driven primarily by the two largest manufacturing sub-sectors, such as vehicle manufacturing and computer, electronic and optical product manufacturing. However, production declined across most other manufacturing sub-sectors, including the manufacture of electrical equipment and food, beverage and tobacco products. On a seasonally and working-day adjusted monthly basis, industrial output rose by 1.0% in August, following a 1.8% gain in the preceding period. For the January to August period, industrial activity was 3.4% higher than in the corresponding period of the previous year.

### 2026-10-06 14:28:59  #Trading Economics #Economy #Denmark #Manufacturing Production MoM #Importance 1

Danish Manufacturing Output Rebounds in August — Denmark’s manufacturing production rose by 0.9% month-on-month in August 2026, rebounding from an upwardly revised eighteen-month low of a 5.0% drop in the previous month. Output recovered in textiles, clothing, and leather products (6.4% vs -3.8% in July), wood and paper products (3.7% vs -1.6%), petroleum and chemical products (7.9% vs -2.0%), metal and metal products (4.4% vs -12.0%), electronics (3.4% vs -13.6%), and transportation (39.0% vs -17.8%). Moreover, activity increased in the manufacture of electrical equipment (3.8% vs 2.3%) and rubber, plastic, glass, and concrete (2.9% vs 0.3%). Output also declined at a softer pace for food, beverage, and tobacco (-0.6% vs -2.4%) and pharmaceuticals (-1.5% vs -6.0%). On a non-seasonally adjusted annual basis, manufacturing production grew by 6.5% in August, following a downwardly revised 3.4% increase in the preceding period.

### 2026-10-06 14:15:54  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Factory Orders #Importance 2

German Factory Orders Slump on Large-Order Reversal — Germany’s factory orders plunged 10.6% month-on-month in August 2026, reversing an upwardly revised 3.2% increase in the prior month and far exceeding market expectations for a 1.0% decline. It was the first monthly drop since April, largely reflecting a 61.5% tumble in orders for aircraft, ships, trains and military vehicles after these surged more than twofold in July on an exceptionally high volume of large contracts. Demand weakened across capital goods (-15.3%), intermediate goods (-2.6%) and consumer goods (-7.3%). Domestic orders fell 17.3%, while foreign demand declined 5.4%, with orders from both the euro area (-5.4%) and non-euro area countries (-5.5%) decreasing. Excluding large-scale contracts, orders edged down just 0.1%. On a less volatile three-month basis, factory orders fell 1.3% in June-August from the previous three months, or 2.6% excluding large orders, pointing to continued weakness in underlying industrial demand.

### 2026-10-06 14:10:07  #Trading Economics #Economy #Romania #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Romania Retail Sales Rebound After Yearlong Decline — Romania’s retail sales increased 0.1% year-on-year in August 2026, marking the first increase after twelve consecutive months of decline, compared with a 6.1% drop in the previous month. The rebound was led by non-food product sales, which increased (0.2% vs -6.7% in July), while sales of food, beverages and tobacco declined (-1.9% vs -6.0%) and retail sales of automotive fuel in specialized stores also fell (-0.3% vs -4.8%), albeit at a softer pace. On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, retail trade rose 2.3%, accelerating from a 0.8% increase in the previous month. In the first eight months of the year, retail sales declined 5.1% from the same period a year earlier.

### 2026-10-06 14:01:21  #MKT News #Market Regions #Europe & UK

【Spain plans new data‑centre regulations before election】Spain will push new data‑centre regulations through an emergency procedure ahead of national elections, with draft rules expected to be submitted to the cabinet in late October or early November. The measures, aimed at large cloud projects by Amazon, Microsoft and Google, would tighten requirements on power and grid connections, including a requirement that data centres use renewable electricity for 80% of operating hours and demonstrate energy sources on an hourly basis. The government says the rules are intended to curb rising electricity demand and prevent cost pass‑through to households and industry. The regulation would not need parliamentary approval; the CNMC has issued a favourable opinion and the draft will go to the Council of State for legal review. Industry sources expect the final text may be softened from the initial proposal.

【餘承東：記憶體漲價每部手機多花200美元】10月6日，華為海外 X 賬號釋出了 9 月 29 日的國際媒體圓桌會議摘要。華為常務董事、產品投資評審委員會主任、終端 BG 董事長餘承東與多家國際媒體的記者就華為消費者業務及 HarmonyOS 生態系統進行了交流。在談到記憶體成本上漲話題時，餘承東坦言，記憶體元件價格的急劇上漲導致每部手機額外增加了超過 200 美元（現匯率約合 1343 元人民幣）成本。由於華為的手機記憶體配置比其他手機更高，成本上漲也更多。 “總體來說，這無疑是整個行業面臨的巨大挑戰。我們看到許多手機製造商在提高價格。我們以非常緩慢的速度漲價，因為我們試圖自己承擔成本，這樣消費者就不必承擔這些成本。但說實話，我們的整體利潤率正在急劇下降。展望未來，我們也不得不（have to）提高價格。如果我們遭受損失，我們的業務將無法持續，而公司必須先能夠生存下來。” 餘承東還提到，智慧手機價格上漲後，低端市場將更加受壓，這將推動廠商向中端和高階市場移動。“高階市場是華為擅長的領域。我們有信心在本次智慧手機價格上漲中繼續參賽，並將繼續為消費者提供更優質的創新產品、體驗和服務。” 餘承東還表示，在歐洲等市場，手機主要透過電信公司銷售。如果華為想在一些發達國家賣出更多手機，就必須迴歸電信轉售渠道。這是華為正在考慮的一個可能性。

ASX 200 Rises 0.6% at Finish — The ASX 200 advanced 49 points or 0.6% to close at 8,736 on Tuesday, marking a third straight gain as stronger U.S. stock futures offset pressure from rising Treasury yields. Traders also digested fresh U.S. economic data while awaiting minutes from the Fed’s September meeting. Locally, sentiment was lifted by Australia's job ads rising for a fifth month last month, underscoring resilient labour demand despite the RBA’s rate hikes. However, the upside was limited by consumer confidence falling for a second month in October, reflecting higher fuel and borrowing costs. Commercial services, healthcare, and financials led the advance, with heavyweight BHP Group up 1.5%, while Rio Tinto added 1.4%. In the meantime, the big four banks added between 0.1% and 0.9%. In contrast, Codan Ltd. fell 4.7%, while tech lagged with Wisetech Global down 2.9%, Nextdc off 2.5%, and Technology One slipping 1.9%. Investors now look to September industry updates and final August building permits.

【《人民日報》連發十文，“新”“實”“深”三大特質彰顯中國經濟的銳氣、靜氣與底氣】2026年，中國經濟來到了新的歷史節點。“十五五”開局之年，中國經濟怎麼樣？面向未來，中國經濟怎麼看？這是時代之問，也是市場之問、信心之問。自9月25日起，《人民日報》連續刊發十篇署名“金軒”的文章，從北斗到生物製造，從智慧經濟到現代交通，從物流變革到高水平安全，從服務業到新場景，從民營企業到強大國內市場，系統闡述了中國經濟的新機遇、新空間、新動能。十篇連發，力度、規格均屬罕見，表面看是十個領域的深度剖析，深層看則完整揭示了中國經濟高質量發展的三大特質，即產業向新、底座向實、改革向深。正是這三大特質的協同推進，構成了中國經濟的長期底氣與發展信心。十篇文章的產業篇，沿著這一邏輯展開。2025年北斗產業總產值突破6290億元，全鏈條自主可控，生物製造在醫藥、化工、能源、食品領域加速滲透，人工智慧核心產業規模連續多年保持20%以上增長，服務業規模總量2025年達80.9萬億元⋯⋯這些不是孤立的技術突破，而是新質生產力從“技術變數”轉化為“經濟增量”的生動註腳。中國經濟的底氣，正在於新產業已經看得見、摸得著、算得出。（每經）

【鈴木以低於比亞迪的價格攪動日本微型電動車市場】日本鈴木汽車公司釋出了其首款純電動車型"e SKY"，屬於日本最舉足輕重的K-car（輕自動車）細分市場，令該領域的競爭進一步升溫。鈴木週二表示，算上政府補貼後，"e SKY"將於11月開售，售價約為160萬日元（約合10,120美元）。這一價格低於日產的暢銷電動車型Sakura，也低於比亞迪今年7月推出的首款K-car Racco。K-car是價格親民、車身極小的車型，佔日本汽車銷量三分之一以上。"e SKY"的加入進一步表明，這類微型電動車正使日本成為各品牌競逐的關鍵戰場——目前日產、本田、大發以及如今的鈴木均已推出電動K-car。日本品牌合計佔據約80%的市場份額。

### 2026-10-06 13:07:06  #Trading Economics #Economy #India #Services PMI #Importance 1

India Services PMI Revised Downward — The HSBC India Services PMI was revised lower to 55.2 in September 2026 from the preliminary estimate of 55.8, after a final reading of 54.1 in the previous month and below the market forecast of 56.2. Still, it marked the strongest growth in the services sector since June, supported by stronger output growth amid robust demand. New orders rose at the fastest pace in three months, supported by marketing efforts and stronger demand in financial and insurance services. Foreign demand also improved, though growth eased to a moderate pace that was the softest in nearly three years. In response to improving new orders, firms increased employment, though the pace moderated and was softer than in August. On the price front, input prices rose, mainly driven by higher food supplies, fuel, and insurance premiums. Meanwhile, output inflation eased to a three-month low. Looking ahead, business sentiment improved to a three-month high, supported by resilient demand and rising customer enquiries.

【日企開發出純電汽車行駛中無線充電技術】據報道，日本本田公司與大成建設集團5日宣佈，雙方共同開發出一項基礎技術，可透過無線方式向行駛中的純電動汽車（EV）輸送電力實現充電。如果該技術得以應用，將使需要停車充電的情況減少，提高純電汽車的便利性。雙方預計將於2027年度以後在千葉縣的館山自動車道開展實證試驗。 此次開發的技術採用了從鋪設在地面的線圈向行駛中的EV供電的方式。本田技術研究所利用自主技術開發出從道路向EV輸送電力的“供電單元”。與普通系統相比，該技術可簡化佈線，縮減埋設範圍。大成建設集團正在對本田技術研究所開發的系統在道路上的施工方法以及供電能力等進行調研。據測算，車輛以約80公里時速在供電單元上行駛的短暫時間內，有望瞬間獲得最大150千瓦的電力供應。

India Composite PMI Revised Lower — India’s HSBC Composite PMI stood at 55.9 in September 2026, down from a flash reading of 56.6 but up from 54.6 in the previous month. The latest result marked the highest level since June, although the average for the second fiscal quarter was the lowest since Q1 2022. New order growth accelerated, but its quarterly performance remained the weakest since the end of fiscal 2021/22, pointing to softer underlying demand momentum. Meanwhile, the resumption of job creation in manufacturing, alongside sustained employment growth among service providers, lifted overall private-sector employment. Price pressures also eased, with firms reporting the weakest increases in input costs in nine months and output charges in three months, suggesting a moderation in inflationary pressures.

Sensex Extends Gains to Second Day — India’s BSE Sensex rose about 0.3% to 72,606 on Tuesday, extending gains for a second day as banks and financial stocks advanced on upbeat quarterly updates, while investors awaited the Reserve Bank of India’s policy decision, with a 25-basis-point rate hike widely expected. Banking stocks led gains, with Kotak Mahindra Bank gained about 3.5% after reporting 24.7% year-on-year growth in net advances, while Axis Bank rose around 1.5% on a 20.7% increase in deposits and 22.7% growth in gross advances. Meanwhile, Trent surged about 9.3% after the apparel retailer projected a 23% year-on-year increase in standalone revenue for the September quarter. Sentiment was also supported by lower oil prices and reduced expectations of aggressive US monetary tightening following weaker US jobs data. However, the rupee remained under pressure as higher US Treasury yields and a stronger dollar weighed on the currency, with the Reserve Bank of India intervening to slow its decline.

【國家稅務總局釋出全國統一的稅務行政處罰裁量基準】近日，國家稅務總局釋出《全國稅務行政處罰裁量基準（2026年版）》，並將於11月1日起施行。這標誌著稅務行政處罰裁量基準實現全國範圍統一。國家稅務總局政策法規司有關負責人介紹，在充分吸收此前各區域實踐成果的基礎上，此次稅務總局對9類66項稅務行政處罰事項裁量階次、適用條件、具體標準進行了全面最佳化，推動實現稅務違法行為事項劃分更加合理、裁量因素設定更加科學、裁量尺度把握更加精確，有利於統一稅務執法標準、規範稅務執法行為、提高稅務執法確定性。國家稅務總局一併釋出了稅務行政處罰“輕微不罰”事項清單，涵蓋從稅務行政處罰裁量基準中歸納的8項行政處罰事項。同時，對各地稅務機關落實全國統一的稅務行政處罰裁量基準提出明確要求，強調要嚴格依法依規實施處罰，在裁量基準範圍內作出處罰決定，保障納稅人繳費人合法權益。（新華社）

Palm Oil Inches Up as Traders Await Monthly Data — Malaysian palm oil futures edged higher to near MYR 4,580 per tonne, extending gains after a rally snapped a recent slide. Support came from a weaker ringgit and firmer crude oil amid Middle East supply risks, lifting palm oil’s appeal as a biodiesel feedstock. Meanwhile, analyst Dorab Mistry sees prices holding between MYR 4,500–5,000 through December, capped by elevated inventories despite El Niño supply risks next year. In India, palm oil imports in the world’s largest importer are expected to stay steady in 2026/27 after a duty cut, with lower edible-oil tariffs ahead of the festive season offering near-term support. Gains were capped by weaker Chicago soyoils, while China’s Dalian exchange stayed shut for a holiday. Caution also grew ahead of Malaysian Palm Oil Board monthly data, with stocks seen peaking this month. In top grower Indonesia, Danantara Sumberdaya began testing a monitoring platform aimed at improving transparency in commodity exports, including palm oil.

【洲際交易所（ICE）在倫敦推出四大貴金屬期貨，力奪全球定價權與衍生品市場】據報道，洲際交易所（ICE）在倫敦正式推出了掛鉤每日拍賣價格的貴金屬每日合約。除黃金外，該合約還全面覆蓋了白銀、鉑金和鈀金。這是在該全球實物金條交易中心引入貴金屬衍生品的最新嘗試，旨在透過中央清算模式提升市場透明度，並與芝商所（CME）等國際巨頭展開正面競爭。全品種覆蓋： ICE 本週在倫敦市場不僅推出了黃金期貨合約，還同步推出了白銀、鉑金和鈀金合約。直接掛鉤拍賣價： 這些合約的定價基礎非常特殊，直接掛鉤由 ICE 本身在倫敦金融城運營的每日實物拍賣價格。這意味著合約的價格生成與倫敦作為全球現貨定價標準的地位高度繫結。期限與清算結構： 新合約的範圍涵蓋從單日到未來六個月不等，且全部透過 ICE 旗下的倫敦分支機構（ICE Clear Europe）進行中央清算。值得一題的是，該清算分支同時管理著全球油價風向標——布倫特原油期貨合約。

【BMI上調2026、2027年亞洲液化天然氣價格預期，因霍爾木茲供應緊張】根據10月5日釋出的報告，BMI將其2026年全年亞洲JKM液化天然氣現貨價格預測從每百萬英熱單位12.5美元上調至15.5美元。 該機構分析師在報告中表示，對2027年的價格預期已上調，由此前的8美元/百萬英熱單位調整為10美元/百萬英熱單位。 此次價格上漲反映了比預期更長時間的供應中斷以及2027年更大的溢位影響。 根據其核心觀點，BMI預計卡達和阿聯酋的氣流將在2026年第四季度及2027年第一季度恢復正常。 “然而，初步達成的霍爾木茲協議可能性低於今年夏季初期的預期，導致價格風險偏向上行，而海灣地區氣量風險則偏向下行。”分析師指出。 BMI預測，荷蘭TTF天然氣價格在2026年平均為每兆瓦時44.0歐元，2027年為36.0歐元，但整體風險仍明顯傾向於上行。

Copper Gains on Risk-On Mood — Copper futures rose to around $6.6 per pound on Tuesday, advancing for a third consecutive session amid a rally in risk assets, highlighted by gains in technology and artificial intelligence stocks. Demand for copper is benefiting from the rapid expansion of data centers and power infrastructure required to support AI development. Still, the near-term demand outlook remains constrained by signs of weakening industrial activity in China, the world’s largest consumer of the metal. On the supply side, Chilean copper output dropped in August to its lowest level since February 2011, while workers at Antofagasta’s Centinela mine voted in favor of strike action after wage negotiations failed to reach an agreement. Meanwhile, the Trump administration has yet to decide on potential tariffs targeting refined copper. Earlier threats of US duties on refined metals encouraged traders to divert shipments toward American warehouses, contributing to the recent surge in copper prices.

### 2026-10-06 11:48:21  #Trading Economics #Economy #Thailand #Inflation Rate #Importance 2

Thailand Inflation Picks Up in September — Thailand’s annual inflation rate rose to 2.82% in September 2026 from 2.53% in August, but came in below market expectations of 3.10%. The reading marked the highest level since April, bringing inflation closer to the upper end of the Bank of Thailand’s 1%-3% target range. The acceleration was driven largely by higher oil and processed food prices, reflecting elevated raw material costs and the impact of the conflict in the Middle East. Meanwhile, annual core inflation rose to 1.50% from 1.44% in August, reaching its highest level since May 2023 but remaining slightly below market forecasts of 1.54%. Headline inflation is expected to accelerate further in the fourth quarter, with October’s rate projected to rise from September’s level, Trade Policy and Strategy Office chief Nantapong Chiralerspong said. He added that recent flooding is expected to have only a limited impact on inflation. The Commerce Ministry expects headline inflation to average 1.80%-2.20% this year.

Uganda Business Confidence Weakens in September — Uganda’s Business Tendency Index fell to 55.8 in September 2026 from a downwardly revised 57.7 in August. Assessments deteriorated across most indicators, including the present business situation (53.4 vs 55.9 in August), order volumes with suppliers (56.5 vs 65.2), current employment (51.6 vs 53), financial situation (51.9 vs 53.2), access to credit (40.4 vs 41.7), current demand for services (55 vs 56.7), future demand for services (72.1 vs 72.5), and order books (40.8 vs 49.5). In addition, average selling prices increased (59.9 vs 58), while competition among businesses also intensified (72.2 vs 70.5). Meanwhile, the outlook for business conditions over the next three months improved (60.5 vs 59.8), as did expected employment (56.7 vs 55.4). Across sectors, confidence weakened in construction (47.7 vs 48.8), manufacturing (53 vs 54.8), wholesale trade (49.3 vs 54.1), agriculture (53.3 vs 56.3), and other services (59 vs 60.3), while it rose in financial services (85.5 vs 80.2).

Meanwhile, foreign equity outflows and persistent importer demand for dollar hedges continued to weigh on the currency, with Indian companies booking a record $77 billion in currency hedges in September, up more than 80% year-on-year. ### 2026-10-06 11:30:36  #Trading Economics #Economy #Jordan #GDP Annual Growth Rate #Importance 0

Jordan GDP Growth Accelerates in Q2 — Jordan’s economy expanded 3.0% year-on-year in the second quarter of 2026, accelerating from 2.8% in the same period a year earlier and from 2.9% in the previous quarter. Economic activity was led by agriculture, forestry and fishing, which grew 7.8%, followed by manufacturing at 6.2%, electricity, gas, steam and air conditioning supply at 5.0%, and water supply, sewerage, waste management and remediation activities at 4.1%. In terms of contributions to overall GDP growth, manufacturing remained the largest contributor, adding 1.04 percentage points, followed by agriculture at 0.34 percentage points and wholesale and retail trade at 0.25 percentage points. Manufacturing also remained the largest sector of the economy, accounting for 17.2% of GDP, ahead of real estate activities at 12.2% and public administration and defense at 7.9%.

TAIEX Pulls Back From Record High on Profit-Taking — The TAIEX, Taiwan’s benchmark stock index, fell 185 points, or 0.4%, to 49,527 in Tuesday morning trade, snapping a four-session winning streak as traders took profits after the index hit a fresh record high a day earlier. However, the broader index remained above the 49,000 level, marking the second straight session above 49,000 intraday after closing at 49,712 in the previous session. The decline was driven by losses in electronic technology (-0.4%), after rising 2.9% the previous day. Losses were also seen in process industries (-0.9%) and communications (-0.7%). TSMC, the world's largest contract chipmaker and accounting for more than 40% of the market's total value, edged down 0.2%, reversing a 3.0% surge on Monday. Meanwhile, MediaTek plunged 4.7%, Unimicron Tech fell 1.9%, and United Microelectronics shed 3.9%. Traders also awaited the FOMC minutes later this week. However, an upbeat session on Wall Street overnight, driven by a rally in tech stocks, limited the fall.

New Zealand Dollar Struggles Near 11-Month Low — The New Zealand dollar steadied around $0.560 after slipping below the level to a fresh low since November 2025 in the previous session. The currency found limited support from data showing business confidence improved in the third quarter, with a net 43% of firms expecting general business conditions to improve in the coming months, up from a net 8% in the previous quarter, despite higher fuel prices and persistent geopolitical risks. However, a stronger US dollar amid elevated Treasury yields continued to weigh on the kiwi. Uncertainty ahead of the November general election has also added to the headwinds. Currently, markets are pricing in around a 58% chance of a 25-basis-point rate hike to 3.0% by the Reserve Bank of New Zealand later this month, while a December move remains more than fully priced in.

### 2026-10-06 09:44:36  #公司

【趙長鵬稱福布斯估值虛高】近日，《紐約時報》刊發幣安創始人趙長鵬的深度專訪。採訪中，趙長鵬圍繞個人財富估值、早期投資邏輯、行業生態現狀與監管方向，輸出一系列觀點。 針對福布斯估算其身家超1140億美元，趙長鵬提出糾偏。他表示，該估值存在明顯虛高，自身真實資產規模大約在100億至300億美元區間。在他看來，財富存在邊際效用遞減，僅需1000萬美元就足以支撐舒適的日常生活，超出該數額的財富，對個人生活品質提升意義有限。 回顧個人投資起點，趙長鵬在採訪中披露，2013年，他在上海一場牌局中首次聽聞比特幣。經過數月研究後，他賣掉上海一套三居室房產，將90萬美元售房款全部投入比特幣。當時他判斷，比特幣代表無國界的金融技術，契合跨境金融長期趨勢，即便投資失利，他仍有能力在傳統金融領域重新獲得高薪工作。 專訪提及MGX對幣安20億美元的投資交易，這筆交易對幣安估值750億美元。趙長鵬原本希望這筆交易以比特幣結算，但阿聯酋投資方擔心比特幣價格劇烈波動帶來交易風險，傾向採用穩定幣完成支付。市場曾有報道稱幣安指定使用當時尚無市場驗證的USD1穩定幣結算，趙長鵬對此予以否認，稱該選擇是MGX自主做出。他就此表達了對穩定幣的看法：只要穩定幣具備良好信譽、不會一夜之間歸零，他便不會過多糾結具體品種。 在公司治理層面，趙長鵬強調，他不再參與幣安的日常運營與管理，僅作為被動股東。對於幣安此前起訴《華爾街日報》一事，趙長鵬直言這場訴訟耗費巨大，即便勝訴，幣安也很難獲得實質收益，屬於不必要的消耗。幣安後續已經撤回該項訴訟。 （東方財經）

### 2026-10-06 09:40:53  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #WTI #Impact bullish #Brent

【Hormuz crude flows recovering; diesel remains severely scarce】Kpler data show average crude transits through the Strait of Hormuz were 10.3 mln bpd over the past seven days, about 76% of the pre-war baseline. Refined product flows (gasoline and diesel) totaled only 1.3 mln bpd, roughly 11% of shipments versus >20% pre-war, signalling sustained diesel tightness. Sources say U.S. oil industry executives have urged the U.S. Navy to prioritise escorting product tankers carrying diesel over crude tankers because crude prices are roughly half those of diesel. So far most U.S.-escorted vessels have been VLCCs (~2 mln-barrel capacity), about double the capacity of the largest product tankers.

### 2026-10-06 09:19:31  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China #CSI300 #Impact bullish #Hang Seng Index #CNH

【Shift from single-network construction to system networking to boost six-network infrastructure efficiency】The Investment Department of the National Development and Reform Commission said China’s current six-networks push is moving from ‘single-point’ buildouts to system-level networking to unlock multiplier effects across water, new power grids, compute, next-generation communications, urban underground utilities and logistics networks. The aim is demand integration and resource sharing so investment yields whole-system gains rather than a simple sum of single networks. Remaining frictions include non‑uniform technical standards, weak cross‑entity coordination and unclear revenue-sharing. The NDRC plans to tackle these by establishing unified technical standards and data interfaces to enable facility sharing and data interoperability; improving market-based coordination, clarifying cross-network roles and revenue allocation and supporting corporate consortia in integrated projects; and running regional integrated pilots in major city clusters to develop replicable models before national rollout.

### 2026-10-06 09:19:23  #Trading Economics #Economy #Philippines #Inflation Rate #Importance 2

Philippines Inflation Rate Jumps to 7.2% — The annual inflation rate in the Philippines accelerated to 7.2% in September 2026 from 6.1% in August, overshooting forecasts of 6.6% and matching April’s three-year high. Main upward pressure came from transport inflation, which rose to 14.6% from 13.5%, while housing, water, electricity, gas and other fuels increased to 8.4% from 7.9%, as the country’s heavy reliance on Middle Eastern oil left it particularly exposed to higher energy costs amid the regional conflict. Food and non-alcoholic beverage inflation also rose to 6.7% from 4.6%, while inflation for restaurants and accommodation services rose to 7% from 6.8%. Meanwhile, core inflation jumped to 4.7%, the highest since November 2023, above the expected 4.4% and up from 4.1% in August. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 1%, the most since April, exceeding expectations and August’s 0.6% growth.

【從“單點建網”轉向“系統組網” 六網協同如何釋放乘數效應】這一輪 “六張網” 建設從頂層設計階段就將 “協同聯動” 作為核心導向，本質是從 “單點建網” 轉向 “系統組網”，把水網、新型電網、算力網、新一代通訊網、城市地下管網、物流網的需求打通、資源共享，讓投資從 “單張網的簡單相加” 變成 “全體系的乘數效應”，放大基礎設施的整體效能。目前不少領域已經顯現出協同成效。在能源算力領域，新型電網與算力網雙向賦能：算力中心接入綠電直連專案降低用能成本，算力平臺反過來為電網提供智慧調峰服務，部分試點區域綠電消納率提升超15%。在城市執行領域，新一代通訊網為城市地下管網裝上 “數字神經”，實時監測管線狀態，再結合物流網的排程能力，讓城市管線運維、終端物資配送效率提升近兩成。在水利領域，水網與電網、算力網聯動，實現水資源智慧排程與水電協同發電，既強化了供水保障能力，也提高了清潔能源產出效率。從生產端到民生端，六網協同正在把分散的基礎設施擰成一股繩。（央視）

### 2026-10-06 08:59:32  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Gasoline Falls for Third Session — US gasoline futures fell to around $3.30 per gallon, declining for the third straight session, pressured by seasonal weakness in fuel demand and the G7’s planned release of emergency oil and diesel reserves. The release will be coordinated by the International Energy Agency over the next four months. The broader refined-products complex also faced pressure after President Trump signed an executive order expanding access to tax-exempt diesel. Meanwhile, gasoline pump prices have eased from recent peaks as demand weakens following the end of the summer driving season. Nevertheless, global fuel markets remain tight as EIA data showed US gasoline inventories fell by 1.7 million barrels in the week ended September 25, while refinery utilization stood at 92.5%. Russia extended restrictions on most diesel exports through October, although it is considering a partial lift amid balanced domestic supplies. China also suspended some oil-product exports for October.

Silver Pressured as Dollar, Yields Weigh — Silver held near $61 an ounce on Tuesday, remaining close to two-month lows as a stronger dollar and surging Treasury yields outweighed support from softer US jobs data and reduced expectations for an October rate hike by the Federal Reserve. The dollar strengthened largely as the euro weakened amid rising political uncertainty and fiscal concerns across Europe. Treasury yields also climbed to fresh 24-year highs as the global bond selloff continued, driven by mounting fiscal risks and persistent inflation concerns. ISM data showed that cost pressures in the US services sector increased at the fastest pace in more than four years last month. Meanwhile, markets are pricing in roughly a 78% probability that the Fed will keep interest rates unchanged this month following weaker-than-expected US jobs data. Elsewhere, oil prices declined on signs of increased supply from the Middle East, easing concerns over inflation and higher interest rates.

Heating Oil Slips on Tax-Exempt Diesel — US heating oil prices fell to around $4.51 per gallon as President Donald Trump signed an executive order expanding access to tax-exempt diesel in the latest bid to lower diesel costs. The order waives the off-road requirement for red-dyed diesel, allowing it to be purchased tax-free for any use. The G7 also pledged last week to release 100 million barrels of diesel from strategic emergency reserves, although the amount of additional supply remained unclear. Nevertheless, heating oil prices remained close to their record high of $5.20 reached in mid-September as refined product markets remained tight. EIA data showed US distillate inventories at historically low levels, while refinery maintenance and seasonal demand were expected to keep supplies tight. Disruptions in the Middle East and Russia, along with Moscow’s extended diesel-export restrictions and China’s suspension of October fuel exports, further constrained global diesel availability.

### 2026-10-06 08:04:39  #Trading Economics #Economy #Australia #Consumer Confidence MoM #Importance 2

Australia Consumer Sentiment Weakens for 2nd Straight Month — Australia’s Westpac–Melbourne Institute Consumer Sentiment Index fell 4.7% mom to a six-month low of 80.4 in October 2026, slowing from a 5.2% decline in the prior month while marking a second straight month of decline. Confidence remained under pressure from higher fuel and interest rates, as views on household finances weakened, with assessments of finances over the past year falling 8.0% to 66.9, while expectations for the next 12 months declined 6.4% to 88.4. Economic sentiment was also subdued, with the one-year outlook slipping 1.6% to 78.3 and the five-year outlook edging down 0.4% to 85.5. The index measuring whether it was a good time to buy major household items fell 7.1% to 83.0, while unemployment expectations rose 1.9% to 142.1. Westpac economist Matthew Hassan said previously flagged upside risks to inflation were materialising, while the RBA was also wary of demand pressures from the AI and data-centre boom. He expects another rate hike at the RBA’s November meeting.

Futures linked to the Dow, S&P 500 and Nasdaq 100 were each up around 0.1%. In regular trading on Monday, the Nasdaq Composite climbed 1.05% to a fresh record high, while the S&P 500 and Dow advanced 0.66% and 0.18%, respectively. Megacap technology stocks led the gains, with Nvidia rising 2.1%, Tesla adding 2.2% and Microsoft gaining 1.5%. SpaceX also jumped more than 7% following reports that the company has proposed building a natural gas pipeline in Florida to support Starship launches from Cape Canaveral. Meanwhile, US 10- and 30-year Treasury yields climbed to their highest levels since 2002, keeping investors cautious. Elsewhere, oil prices fell on signs of increased supply from the Middle East, easing concerns over inflation and the prospect of higher interest rates.

Australian stocks Rise for 3rd Session — Australian equities rose 39 points, or 0.4%, to 8,725 in Tuesday morning trade, marking a third straight gain after Wall Street closed higher overnight on AI optimism and buyout activity, even as Treasury yields hovered near 5.31%. Locally, the Melbourne Institute’s September Inflation Gauge added 0.3% mom, marking the smallest rise in three months and suggesting tighter policy is cooling price pressures though risks remain. Commercial services led advances, with consumer non-durables, consumer services, and healthcare also firm. Gains were tempered, however, as October consumer sentiment in Australia stayed in negative territory, though the pace of decline eased despite higher fuel and borrowing costs. Standout movers included PLS Group (4.4%), Bluescope Steel (2.4%), Capricorn Metals (2.6%), and Scentre Group (2.1%). Traders now look to September job ads data later today, while Chinese markets return Thursday after the Golden Week holiday.

Brent Holds Losses as Mideast Exports Recover — Brent crude traded near $100 per barrel on Tuesday after declining in the previous session, pressured by signs that Middle East crude exports are recovering toward pre-war levels. JPMorgan said crude shipments have rebounded to 17.5 million barrels a day, or 98% of pre-war levels, while product flows such as diesel and gasoline reached 3 million barrels a day, or 58%. Gulf producers are moving larger volumes through the Strait of Hormuz despite elevated shipping risks, with Iraq seeking to hire additional vessels to transport its cargoes through the waterway. Kuwait also said it is pumping oil at around 75% of the level seen before the Iran war. In addition, Saudi Arabia sharply reduced the price of its flagship crude grade for Asian buyers as supply flows improved, further signaling a loosening market. Meanwhile, investors are awaiting the US EIA’s Short-Term Energy Outlook, due later today, for fresh insights into the US energy market.

### 2026-10-06 07:27:25  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 2

Oil Holds Losses as Mideast Exports Recover — Crude oil traded near $89 per barrel on Tuesday after declining for two consecutive sessions, pressured by signs that Middle East crude exports are recovering toward pre-war levels. JPMorgan said crude shipments have rebounded to 17.5 million barrels a day, or 98% of pre-war levels, while product flows such as diesel and gasoline reached 3 million barrels a day, or 58%. Gulf producers are moving larger volumes through the Strait of Hormuz despite elevated shipping risks, with Iraq seeking to hire additional vessels to transport its cargoes through the waterway. Kuwait also said it is pumping oil at around 75% of the level seen before the Iran war. In addition, Saudi Arabia sharply reduced the price of its flagship crude grade for Asian buyers as supply flows improved, further signaling a loosening market. Meanwhile, investors are awaiting the US EIA’s Short-Term Energy Outlook, due later today, for fresh insights into the US energy market.

【蘋果需就6.34億美元專利敗訴款支付1.84億美元利息】聯邦法官週一裁定，蘋果公司需額外支付1.84億美元利息。該判決源於陪審團作出的6.34億美元專利損害賠償裁決，因搭載心率監測功能的蘋果手錶侵權，判決賠付給馬西莫公司（MasimoCorp.）。詹姆斯・V・塞爾納法官駁回蘋果的主張，蘋果認為判付利息會讓馬西莫獲得意外之財。法官援引美國最高法院的指導意見：“自專利許可費本應收到之日起計算利息，僅用於讓專利權人恢復至無侵權時的完整經濟狀態。”法官沒有采納馬西莫提出的7%利率，而是採用侵權行為始於2020年時馬西莫自身的借款利率，按年複利計息。加州中區聯邦地區法院的這項裁定同時要求，蘋果需要針對陪審團賠償金加上判決前利息合計8.18億美元，繼續支付判決後利息，直至全額履行判決。

### 2026-10-06 06:56:34  #Trading Economics #Economy #New Zealand #Calendar #Importance 2

New Zealand Business Mood Improves Sharply in Q3 — New Zealand's business confidence surged in Q3 2026, with a net 43% of survey firms expecting general business conditions to improve, up from 8% in Q2. The latest New Zealand Institute of Economic Research's (NZIER) Quarterly Survey of Business Opinion showed that the improvement came amid the ongoing Middle East conflict. A net 40% of firms expect general economic conditions to improve in the coming months, up from 14% in Q2, marking the highest in more than a year. Businesses were more optimistic about demand in the next quarter, with 15% expecting better trading, up from 11% in the previous survey. Meanwhile, expectations of inflation risks over the coming year also improved, with the share of firms expecting higher costs falling to 47% from 54%, while those expecting to increase prices eased to 43% from 54%. However, the survey suggested flat economic growth over the quarter, while firms expected the RBNZ to hike interest rates this year.

### 2026-10-06 06:53:47  #Trading Economics #Markets #New Zealand #Stock Market #Importance 1

New Zealand Stocks Edge Lower in Morning Trade — The NZX 50 dropped 26 points, or 0.2%, to 13,673 in Tuesday morning trade, erasing slight gains in the previous session, mainly weighed down by materials, healthcare, and consumer staples. However, gains in financials, energy, and consumer discretionary limited the fall. Easing oil prices also prevented the index from falling further, as did an improvement in business confidence. The latest New Zealand Institute of Economic Research's (NZIER) Quarterly Survey of Business Opinion showed that New Zealand's business confidence improved sharply in Q3 amid the Middle East conflict, with inflation pressures easing, steady profit expectations, and improved hiring and investment. However, traders awaited the FOMC minutes later this week. Among early losers were Delegat Group (-1.4%), A2 Milk (-1.0%), Fisher & Paykel (-0.8%), Contact Energy (-0.6%), and South Port NZ (-0.6%).

South Korea FX Reserves End Three-Month Rising Streak — South Korea’s foreign exchange reserves fell to $440.6 billion at the end of September 2026 from $442.3 billion in August, ending a three-month rising streak. August had recorded the largest monthly gain on record. Securities remained the largest component, falling to $386 billion from $387.1 billion, while deposits declined to $30 billion from $30.3 billion. Special Drawing Rights (SDRs) fell to $15.5 billion from $15.8 billion. Gold holdings and the country’s reserve position at the IMF were unchanged at $4.8 billion and $4.3 billion, respectively.

【Wall Street syndicate launches record $60 bln AI chip financing for ANTHROPIC】BofA, CITIGROUP and MORGAN STANLEY have started syndicating $60 bln of debt to finance ANTHROPIC’s leasing of Google AI chips, the largest chip financing to date. About $42 bln is a Broadcom-backed senior secured loan whose syndication began Monday; a $18 bln subordinated tranche without Broadcom backing will be launched later, with Blackstone committed to roughly $9 bln. Proceeds will fund ANTHROPIC’s 2027 chip orders and lease payments will begin after delivery. Broadcom may also receive up to $42 bln of convertible notes to cover related lease fees. The transaction is being treated as a barometer of market appetite for AI-related debt.

Ibovespa Closes at a Record High — The Ibovespa surged 7.7% to a record 206,912 on Monday, its biggest one-day gain in over six years, after Flávio Bolsonaro finished ahead of President Lula in the first election round, defying most polls. The result triggered a sharp repricing of Brazilian assets. Bolsonaro received 47.03% of valid votes, compared with 45.16% for President Lula. The senator is viewed by markets as more fiscally restrictive, amid elevated domestic yields and weak business activity. Bolsonaro outperformed the average of polls tracked by Goldman Sachs by more than four percentage points. Gains were broad-based, with financials leading the index. Itaú soared 10.4%, Bradesco jumped 13.6%, BB rose 11% and B3 surged 22.6%. Other outperformers included Sabesp (11.6%) and Petrobras (8.2%). JPMorgan upgraded Brazil to Buy. Banks expect the index to extend its record-setting streak in upcoming sessions. The Ibovespa also set a record for trading volume, with more than 5 million trades during the session.

US Stocks Gain as Big Tech Shares Rally — US stock indices closed higher on Monday as a rally in heavyweight technology shares outweighed concerns over still-elevated oil prices and bond yields. The S&P 500 rose 0.7%, the Nasdaq 100 gained 0.9% to close at a record 31,076 and the Dow added 91 points. Markets have absorbed rising borrowing costs, high energy costs and inflation concerns, with stocks continuing to advance. Strong earnings, consumer spending and sustained artificial intelligence-related investment have supported equities. AI hyperscalers posted solid gains, with Alphabet up 0.9%, Meta adding 1.9%, and Microsoft rising 1.5%. Meanwhile, chipmakers closed mixed, with gains in Nvidia (+2.1%) and Broadcom (+2.1%) mostly offsetting losses from Micron (-1%), AMD (-0.3%) and Intel (-2.6%). Tesla rose 2.2%, while SpaceX jumped 7.6% following reports of potential cooperation between Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing and Elon Musk’s Terafab initiative.

Uruguay Inflation Rises in September — Uruguay’s annual inflation rate rose to 4.68% in September 2026 from 4.55% in August. The largest price increases were recorded in education (7.29%), transport (7.13%), healthcare (6.72%), restaurants and lodging (6.64%), and personal care and social security (5.66%). Food and non-alcoholic beverages increased 4.26%. In contrast, clothing and footwear fell 6.21%, making it the only category to record deflation. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.54%, bringing year-to-date inflation to 4.21%.

### 2026-10-06 01:28:49  #Trading Economics #Markets #Brazil #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Brazil 10-Year Yield Nears 1-Year Low — Brazil’s 10-year government bond yield fell to 13%, reaching a near one year low after Flávio Bolsonaro finished ahead of President Lula in the first round of the presidential election, defying most polls. The result triggered a sharp repricing of Brazilian assets. Senator Bolsonaro received 47.03% of valid votes, compared with 45.16% for President Lula. Bolsonaro outperformed the average of polls tracked by Goldman Sachs by more than four percentage points. The senator is viewed by markets as more fiscally restrictive, amid elevated domestic yields and weak business activity. The result strengthened expectations that a Bolsonaro administration could pursue tighter control of public spending and measures to improve Brazil’s deteriorating fiscal position. Lower pressure on public finances could reduce the risk premium demanded by investors, while contractionary fiscal policy could ease inflationary pressures. These factors could allow the BCB to cut interest rates more quickly.

CAC 40 Falls on Fiscal Concerns and Political Risks — The CAC 40 fell 0.8% to close at 7,834 on Monday, hitting a 7-month low on concerns over France’s political and fiscal outlook. Opposition parties remain reluctant to compromise with President Macron’s outgoing administration ahead of next year’s election, with far-right candidate Marine Le Pen and far-left Jean-Luc Mélenchon expected to advance to the runoff. Meanwhile, the president and chief investment strategist at Yardeni Research said Sunday that France could be nearing a full-blown debt crisis. The government recently unveiled a €54 billion fiscal effort to rein in spending and reduce the deficit to 5% of GDP in 2027 from 5.4% this year. Schneider Electric shares tumbled 10% after announcing plans to acquire PTC in the largest deal in the company’s history, raising concerns over valuation, financing and its ability to deliver promised synergies. RBC downgraded PTC to Sector Perform from Outperform. Other laggards included Hermes (-1%), Sanofi (-1.2%) and Legrand (-1%).

Colombian Exports Post Sharpest Growth Since May — Colombia’s exports rose 15.1% year over year to USD 4.46 billion in August 2026, accelerating from a 5.9% increase in July and marking the sharpest growth since May. The result was mainly driven by a 127% surge in exports from the other sectors group, reflecting higher sales of non-monetary gold. Exports of fuels and extractive industry products increased 23.8%, led by higher coal, coke and briquette exports (99.4%) and petroleum, petroleum products and related products (12.3%). Manufacturing exports rose 5.6%, driven by chemicals and related products (18.7%) and miscellaneous manufactured articles (1.9%). In contrast, exports of agricultural, food and beverage products fell 16%, mainly due to lower exports of unroasted coffee (-23.5%) and coffee extracts, essences and concentrates (-51.4%).

### 2026-10-05 23:11:22  #Trading Economics #Economy #Tunisia #Inflation Rate #Importance 0

Tunisia Inflation Rate Hits Near 1-1/2-Year High — Tunisia’s annual inflation rate rose to 5.6% in September 2026 from 5.2% in August, its highest level since April 2025 and the second consecutive monthly increase. Food and non-alcoholic beverages led the increase, with inflation accelerating to 8.4% from 7.5%. Prices increased notably for poultry meat and fresh fruit (16.5% each), sheep meat (14.6%), beef (13%), vegetables (12.9%) and fresh fish (10.4%). Additional upward pressure came primarily from clothing & footwear (9.2% vs 9%); restaurants & hotels (6.5% vs 6.6%); education (5.5% vs 5.4%); miscellaneous goods & services (5.1% vs 5.2%) and housing & utilities (4.3% vs 4.3%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices increased by 0.8% in September, after rising by 0.5% in the prior month.

### 2026-10-05 22:40:58  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Cotton Near Two-Month Low — Cotton futures traded around 80 cents per pound, near its lowest level in two months, amid concerns over the weather and its impact on crops. Heavy rainfall across Texas, a key US cotton-growing region, raised concerns over crop quality, particularly where bolls had already opened. However, no further rain is forecast this week, limiting the upside for prices. Providing an offsetting pressure, the US harvest is quickly progressing, with 17% of the crop harvested compared with the historical average of 15%, while Texas and Arizona are running well ahead of their normal pace, according to the latest USDA crop progress report. Adding to the bearish pressure is an intensifying competition in global markets. Brazil exported a record 3.37 million tonnes between August 2025 and July 2026, an increase of 19% from the previous marketing year. US exports reached approximately 2.61 million tonnes over the same period.
